HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B004","root_000531/B005","root_000531/B013"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_128e4b8463f347395597","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:6:3\",\"root_000531\",[\"B005\",\"B004\",\"B013\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sûrenin başında bakması istenen kişi ile burada görülmek isteyenler aynı görme alanında buluşur; gösteriş, seyircinin tepkisine göre ayarlanan karşılıklı bir görünürlük düzenidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_334e28587ae7e9bc64da","text":"3. **Mutual visibility as social theater.** Evidence: `ر ء ي B004` mutual visibility/facing, with `ر ء ي B005` showing off; activated by plural praying group in 107:4-5. Structure: repeated plurals create a scene of people facing and seeing each other. Mechanism: worship becomes a field of reciprocal spectatorship. Abductive move: I infer audience formation from plural morphology and mutual-visibility branch; the packet supplies both. Reading change: the ayah expands from one hypocritical display to a social theater of religious reputation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B001","root_000531/B012"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_553668bf7156748f8f68","text":"2. **Engineered visibility becomes evidence against its engineer.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ر ء ي/B012` is causing another to see or deliberately showing something; fixed `ر ء ي/B001` includes insight as well as eyesight. **Structural cue:** 107:2-107:5 teach the invited viewer what conduct diagnoses denial before 107:6 reveals deliberate self-display. **Assigned role:** showing produces the visible evidence, while insight reads that evidence against the surrounding social record. **Abductive move:** the packet does not state that display self-incriminates; I infer this reversal because the same observer sees both ritual appearance and failures of care. **Reading change:** the attempt to manufacture a favorable view paradoxically makes the disjunction more legible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B011"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_562ff3f612e2c77d19fa","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:6:3\",\"root_000531\",[\"B011\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Namaz, davranışı yöneten ibadet olmaktan çıkıp uzaktan görülen bir kimlik işaretine dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B012"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5a8e73e1335bd0d712c2","text":"107:7 supplied a material control condition for the public display: the identity-marker is visible, but the ordinary benefit is withheld. That later contrast sharpened `ر ء ي/B012` from neutral showing into self-defeating evidence production, because what is made seen can now be tested against what the same agents prevent from reaching others.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5cea47f70795a91caa59","text":"4. **Raised repute versus divine Name.** Evidence: `ر ء ي B005` includes ostentation and reputation, activated by `س م و B008` good repute spread and `س م و B005` name as raised designation. Structure: beginning-name and near-ending display compete. Mechanism: the performer seeks a raised social name through acts that should have been under the divine Name. Abductive move: I supply the competition of reputational elevation; the packet supplies name/repute and showing-off. Reading change: يراءون becomes a counter-basmalah: action done in the name of audience.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_879824666a9ac96447cd","text":"3. **Public opinion replaces accountable judgment.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ر ء ي/B002` is opinion and reflective judgment, and fixed `ر ء ي/B006` is visible appearance or mirror-image; 107:1's `د ي ن/B002` is reckoning and judgment. **Structural cue:** the sequence moves from accountable `الدين` to visible prayer and then to conduct aimed at people's eyes. **Assigned role:** the mirror-image is the managed surface, and human opinion is the substitute tribunal. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies two kinds of judgment but does not explicitly rank them; I infer displacement because the action is optimized for viewers while obligations remain unmet. **Reading change:** `يراءون` names a competing economy of judgment in which appearance before people displaces reckoning for deeds.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B011","root_000879/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a7b8ea2edeaaeae388e9","text":"4. **Prayer is raised as an identity-marker rather than allowed to govern action.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ر ء ي/B011` is a flag or marker set up to be seen; 107:4-107:5 supply `ص ل و/B003`, prescribed worship, and 107:7 supplies `م ن ع/B001`, withholding good. **Structural cue:** the showing-off clause directly modifies the worshippers and is immediately followed by conduct that contradicts the displayed identity. **Assigned role:** the flag branch turns prayer into a public sign of affiliation. **Abductive move:** no literal flag appears; I infer signaling from the marker image and the explicit effort to be seen. **Reading change:** `يراءون` becomes identity display: worship is hoisted as a sign while being denied control over ordinary giving.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B005","root_000531/B013"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b5118328df19bcb828da","text":"1. **The surah closes a reciprocal visibility loop.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ر ء ي/B005` is performing an act so people see it, and fixed `ر ء ي/B004` is mutual visibility and facing; 107:1's `ر ء ي/B013` is the opening “tell me” alert. **Structural cue:** the addressee is first told to look, and the identified people are later said to seek being looked at. **Assigned role:** mutual visibility makes observer and performer two sides of one social circuit. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies both acts of seeing; I infer that the performers select conduct in anticipation of the observer introduced at 107:1. **Reading change:** `يراءون` is not merely private insincerity but calibrated action within a feedback loop of gaze and recognition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c8ed79e380146e6e0880","text":"107:7 retrospectively grounded riya in economics: the performer does not only seek eyes but uses that visible piety while blocking ordinary benefit. Looking back to 107:0, the surprise is that `س م و B008` good repute can be hijacked; public religious visibility becomes a counterfeit raised name.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B002","root_000531/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d25c2c0ac5bf7038e19f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:6:3\",\"root_000531\",[\"B002\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ayna gibi yönetilen dış görünüş ve insanların kanaati, iç değerlendirme ile hesap sorumluluğunun yerine geçer.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d479f1bef980498f3f8d","text":"1. **Public visibility closes the opening question.** Evidence: `ر ء ي B005` showing off before people, activated by `ر ء ي B013` tell-me formula and `ر ء ي B001` seeing/insight in 107:1. Structure: the surah starts by asking the hearer to see and then identifies people who act to be seen. Mechanism: the object of diagnosis manipulates the same visual field used for diagnosis. Abductive move: I supply the recursive visual mechanism; the packet supplies repeated root and ostentation branch. Reading change: يراءون is not an extra vice but the answer to “Have you seen?”","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d908074585374707324f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:6:3\",\"root_000531\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Gösteriş, eylemin yönünü Allah'tan seyircinin vereceği itibara çevirerek ilahî adla başlayan yönelişin karşısına başka bir yöneliş koyar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B006","root_003249/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_dc380d66dd997ca9f8ef","text":"2. **Mirror appearance replacing inner judgment.** Evidence: `ر ء ي B006` visible appearance/mirror and `ر ء ي B002` reflective judgment; activated by `س ه و B001` heart-heedlessness in 107:5. Structure: immediately after inattentive prayer, the visual surface becomes dominant. Mechanism: the mirror-like surface substitutes for inner رأي and attention. Abductive move: I infer displacement from inward judgment to outward appearance; the packet supplies mirror/appearance and heart-judgment branches. Reading change: ostentation reads as epistemic corruption: appearance governs what should have been insight.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B001","root_000531/B012"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_fd20136e03e10535c626","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:6:3\",\"root_000531\",[\"B012\",\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kişi görünüşünü denetlemeye çalıştıkça kendi aleyhindeki kanıtı da görünür kılar; seyirci, sergilenen ibadet ile gizlenemeyen bakım eksikliğini birlikte görür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
